Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The microevolution variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 319 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 246 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the microevolution space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 319 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 246. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^773(S, F) is trivial. QED.
